### Installation
Clone the repository and create an anaconda environment using
```shell
git clone --recursive https://github.com/rongduo/DOFGS_implementation.git
cd DOFGS_implementation

conda env create -f environment.yml
conda activate dofgs
pip install torch==2.0.1+cu118 torchvision==0.15.2+cu118  torchaudio==2.0.2+cu118 -f https://download.pytorch.org/whl/torch_stable.html
pip install torch-cluster -f https://data.pyg.org/whl/torch-2.0.1+cu118.html  
pip install torchmetrics==1.3.0 torchtyping==0.1.4 torch-efficient-distloss==0.1.3
pip install submodules/diff-gaussian-rasterization-dof
pip install submodules/simple-knn
```

### Download dataset
Please download the real defocus dataset from [this url](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1_TkpcJnw504ZOWmgVTD7vWqPdzbk9Wx_) and put the downloaded data under the folder ```./data/DeblurNeRF_data/```.

Plase download the synthetic defocus dataset with COLMAP data from [this url](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1E3ZDelbCZn2ypUtho9C2k0PqCruse8dP?usp=sharing) and put the downloaded data under the folder ```./data/DeblurNeRF_data/```

The file structure of the `./data/` folder should be:

```
data/
├── DeblurNeRF_data/
│   ├── real_defocus_blur/
│   │   ├── defocuscake/
│   │   ├── defocuscaps/
│   │   └── ...
│   └── synthetic_defocus_blur/
│       ├── defocuscozy2room/
│       ├── defocusfactory/
│       └── ...
```

### Run Optimization

To run the optimizer, simply use

```shell
# run on real defocus scenes
sh scripts/train_real.sh
# fun on synthetic defocus scenes:
sh scripts/train_syn.sh
```

### Render and Evaluation
To render and evaluate the reconstructed scene for all scenes, run the following command:
```shell
# render and evaluate on real defocus scenes
sh scripts/val_real.sh
# render and evaluate on synthetic defocus scenes
sh scripts/val_syn.sh
```
After running, the images and numerical results are available in corresponding scene folders.

### Download pre-optimized scenes
You can also directly download the reconstructed scenes for different scenes in the real defocus dataset and synthetic dataset from [this url](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1E3ZDelbCZn2ypUtho9C2k0PqCruse8dP?usp=sharing) and put the decompressed folder in `./output/` folder. Then the `./output/model_dofgs/` folder should be like:

The file structure of the `./output/model_dofgs/` folder should be:

```
output/
└── model_dofgs/
    ├── real_results/           # Results on real defocus dataset at 600×400 resolution
    │   ├── real_cake/
    │   ├── real_caps/
    │   └── ...
    └── synthetic_results/      # Results on synthetic defocus dataset
        ├── synthetic_cozy2room/
        ├── synthetic_factory/
        └── ...
```


Note: The original checkpoints were unfortunately lost during server migration. We have reproduced the models, and the performance may slightly differ from the results reported in the paper.

All of the parameters can be easily specified by editing `config_files/config_xxx.yaml`.
<details>
<summary><strong> Key Configuration Parameters (Click to expand)</strong></summary>

- `model_folder`: Output folder name for the current experiment (e.g., `real_results`)
- `scene_name`: List of scene(s) to be trained or evaluated (e.g., `real_cake`)
- `model_version`: Specifies the directory under `src/` that contains the core module implementation
- `output_path`: The path where output results are saved. This does not need to be specified manually—it will be automatically derived from `model_folder`, `model_version` and `scene_name`
- `source_path`: The path to the data corresponding to `scene_name`.  It does not need to be provided explicitly, as it will be automatically retrieved from `config_files\scene_info.yaml`
- `num_extra_points`: Number of extra points to be added during densification
- `iterations`: Total number of training iterations
- `test_iterations`, `save_iterations`: Iterations at which evaluation and checkpoint saving occur
- `fdist_lr`, `position_lr_init`, `position_lr_final`, `scaling_lr`: Learning rates for focal distance, gaussian attributes.
- `lambda_infocus`, `lambda_dssim`, `lambda_mask_loss`: Loss weights for different supervision signals
- `use_mask_loss`: Whether to apply in-focus mask supervision
- `use_depth_estimator`: Whether to use a depth estimator for supervision (off by default)
- `band_limit`: Whether to apply band-limiting within the gaussian rasterizer
- `kernel_size`: Kernel size used additionally within the gaussian rasterizer 
- `tonemap_type`: Type of tone mapping to apply (e.g., `none`, `gamma`, etc.)
</details>
<br>

### Acknowledgement
Our implementation is primarily based on the official codebase for [3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)](https://github.com/graphdeco-inria/gaussian-splatting). Please follow the license of 3DGS. And we have also drawn inspiration from parts of the [Mip-Splatting](https://github.com/autonomousvision/mip-splatting) implementation. We sincerely thank the authors of both projects for their excellent work and for sharing their codebases with the community.

### render.py
```
import torch
from src.scene import Scene
import os
from tqdm import tqdm
from os import makedirs
import torchvision
from src.utils.general_utils import safe_state
from argparse import ArgumentParser
from src.utils.general_utils import import_class_from_file, load_yaml_file
import sys
from omegaconf import OmegaConf
import omegaconf
import copy
from src.metrics import compute_img_metric
import cv2 
import numpy as np
from functools import partial
import seaborn as sns 


def get_emb(sin_inp):
    """
    Gets a base embedding for one dimension with sin and cos intertwined
    """
    emb = torch.stack((sin_inp.sin(), sin_inp.cos()), dim=-1)
    return torch.flatten(emb, -2, -1)

def get_2d_emb(batch_size, x, y, out_ch, device):
    out_ch = int(np.ceil(out_ch / 4) * 2)
    inv_freq = 1.0 / (10000 ** (torch.arange(0, out_ch, 2).float() / out_ch))
    pos_x = torch.arange(x, device=device).type(inv_freq.type())*2*np.pi/x
    pos_y = torch.arange(y, device=device).type(inv_freq.type())*2*np.pi/y
    sin_inp_x = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", pos_x, inv_freq)
    sin_inp_y = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", pos_y, inv_freq)
    emb_x = get_emb(sin_inp_x).unsqueeze(1)
    emb_y = get_emb(sin_inp_y)
    emb = torch.zeros((x, y, out_ch * 2), device=device)
    emb[:, :, : out_ch] = emb_x
    emb[:, :, out_ch : 2 * out_ch] = emb_y
    return emb[None, :, :, :].repeat(batch_size, 1, 1, 1)


def save_grayimg(img_tensor, save_path, colored=True, cmap=None):
    img_np = img_tensor.squeeze().cpu().numpy()
    img_np = (img_np * 255.0).round().astype(np.uint8)
    if cmap is None:
        cmap = cv2.COLORMAP_JET
    if colored:
        color_img = cv2.applyColorMap(img_np, cmap) 
    else:
        color_img = img_np
    cv2.imwrite(save_path, color_img)
       

def merge_params(conf, args):
    for key, value in args.__dict__.items():
        if key in conf.__dict__['_content'].keys():
            setattr(args, key, getattr(conf, key))
        else:
            setattr(conf, key, value)
        # print(key, getattr(args, key))
    
    for key, value in conf.__dict__['_content'].items():
        if key not in args.__dict__.keys():
            setattr(args, key, value)
    return conf, args


def render_set(model_path, name, iteration, views, gaussians, pipeline, background, tone_map):
    render_path = os.path.join(model_path, name, "ours_{}".format(iteration), "renders_0604")
    gts_path = os.path.join(model_path, name, "ours_{}".format(iteration), "gt")

    makedirs(render_path, exist_ok=True)
    makedirs(gts_path, exist_ok=True)

    valid_F = torch.tensor([1.0], device='cuda') 
    valid_F.unsqueeze(0)
    valid_K = torch.tensor([0.0], device='cuda')
    valid_K.unsqueeze(0)

    psnr_all = 0
    ssim_all = 0
    count = 0

    writer = open(os.path.join(render_path, 'test_metrics.txt'), 'a')
    writer.write('=========================\n')

    if name == 'train':
        # under training mode, we have the in-focus localization network, which uses positional encoding for training views as input
        pos_enc = get_2d_emb(1, views[0].original_image.shape[-2], views[0].original_image.shape[-1], 16, torch.device("cuda:0"))

    for idx in tqdm(range(len(views)), desc="Rendering progress"):
        view = views[idx]
        # print(idx, view.image_name)
        if name == 'train':
            renders = render(view, gaussians, pipeline, background, select_view=idx)
            renders_fake = render(view, gaussians, pipeline, background, valid_F, valid_K)
            rendering = renders["render"]
            rendering_aif = renders_fake["render"]
            rendering_aif = tone_map(rendering_aif)
            if 'depth' in renders.keys():
                depth = renders["depth"]
                depth_aif = renders_fake["depth"]
            if 'coc_alpha' in renders.keys():
                coc_alpha = renders["coc_alpha"]
                coc_a_exp = torch.log1p(coc_alpha)
                coc_a_exp = 1.0 - (coc_a_exp - coc_a_exp.min()) / (coc_a_exp.max() - coc_a_exp.min())
                
                blur_depth = renders["depth"]
                norm_depth = (blur_depth - blur_depth.min()) / (blur_depth.max() - blur_depth.min())
                norm_coc = (coc_alpha - coc_alpha.min()) / (coc_alpha.max()- coc_alpha.min())
                coc_alpha = coc_alpha.squeeze() / coc_alpha.max()
                outfocus_mask = gaussians.mask_net(idx, pos_enc,
                                                    torch.cat([rendering, norm_depth], dim=-3).detach(), norm_coc.detach())
                infocus_mask = 1.0 - outfocus_mask
                
        else:
            renders = render(view, gaussians, pipeline, background, valid_F, valid_K)
            rendering = renders["render"]
            if 'depth' in renders.keys():
                depth = renders["depth"]

        
        rendering = torch.clamp(rendering, 0.0, 1.0)    
        gt = view.original_image[0:3, :, :]

        psnr_cur = compute_img_metric(rendering, gt, 'psnr')
        ssim_cur = compute_img_metric(rendering, gt, 'ssim')

        psnr_all += psnr_cur 
        ssim_all += ssim_cur 
        count += 1

        view_idx = view.image_name
        writer.write(f'Idx: {idx:d}, PSNR: {psnr_cur:.4f}, SSIM: {ssim_cur:.4f}\n')

        # save images
        torchvision.utils.save_image(rendering, os.path.join(render_path, '{}_ours.png'.format(view_idx)))
        torchvision.utils.save_image(gt, os.path.join(gts_path, '{}.png'.format(view_idx)))
        # save depth image 
        if 'depth' in renders.keys():
            depth = depth.squeeze() / depth.max()
            save_grayimg(depth, os.path.join(render_path, "{}_depth.png".format(view_idx)))


        if name == 'train':
            torchvision.utils.save_image(rendering_aif, os.path.join(render_path, "{}-aif.png".format(view_idx)))
            if 'depth' in renders.keys():
                depth_aif = depth_aif.squeeze() / depth_aif.max()
                save_grayimg(depth_aif, os.path.join(render_path, "{}_depth-aif.png".format(view_idx)))
            if 'coc_alpha' in renders.keys():      
                save_grayimg(coc_alpha, os.path.join(render_path, "{}_coc_a.png".format(view_idx)))
                save_grayimg(coc_a_exp, os.path.join(render_path, '{}_coc_exp_normed.png'.format(view_idx)), colored=False)
                save_grayimg(infocus_mask, os.path.join(render_path, '{}_infocus_mask.png').format(view_idx), colored=True, cmap=None)
    
    avg_psnr = psnr_all / count 
    avg_ssim = ssim_all / count 
    
    print(f'Count: {count:d}, Avg PSNR: {avg_psnr:.4f}  Avg SSIM: {avg_ssim:.4f} \n')
    writer.write(f'Count: {count:d}, Avg PSNR: {avg_psnr:.4f}  Avg SSIM: {avg_ssim:.4f} \n')
    writer.close()




def render_sets(GaussianModel, dataset, iteration, pipeline, skip_train : bool, skip_test : bool):
    with torch.no_grad():
        gaussians = GaussianModel(dataset.sh_degree)
        print('dataset.hold ', dataset.hold)
        scene = Scene(dataset, gaussians, load_iteration=iteration, shuffle=False)
        gaussians = scene.gaussians

        
        train_cams = scene.getTrainCameras().copy()
        #gaussians.compute_3D_filter(cameras=train_cams)
        gaussians.setup_defocus_params(int(len(scene.getTrainCameras().copy())), scene.cameras_translate, dataset.source_path)

        
        tonemap_type = getattr(dataset, 'tonemap_type', 'none')
        tone_map = ToneMapping(tonemap_type).cuda()
        print('initialized tonemap function')

        defocus_ckpt = os.path.join(str(dataset.model_path), 'defocus_params', f'iteration_{iteration:d}/defocus_params.pth')
        if os.path.isfile(defocus_ckpt):
            gaussians.load_defocus_params(defocus_ckpt)
            print('load defocus ckpt succesfully')

        bg_color = [1,1,1] if dataset.white_background else [0, 0, 0]
        background = torch.tensor(bg_color, dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")

        if not skip_train:
            render_set(dataset.model_path, "train", scene.loaded_iter, scene.getTrainCameras().copy(), gaussians, pipeline, background, tone_map)

        if not skip_test:
            render_set(dataset.model_path, "test", scene.loaded_iter, scene.getTestCameras().copy(), gaussians, pipeline, background, tone_map)



if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Set up command line argument parser
    parser_config = ArgumentParser(description="argparser for the config file")
    parser_config.add_argument('--scene', nargs="+", type=str, required=True, default=['real_cake'], help='specified which scene to eval and test')
    parser_config.add_argument('--config', type=str, required=True, help='config file')
    args_config = parser_config.parse_args(sys.argv[1:5])  #parse command-line arguments
    
    # load the config file
    conf = OmegaConf.load(args_config.config)
    # print(conf)
    test_scene_list = args_config.scene 
    
    # import the needed argument and model classes 
    ModelParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'ModelParams')
    PipelineParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'PipelineParams')
    OptimizationParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'OptimizationParams')
    get_combined_args = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'get_combined_args')
        
    # load model classes
    GaussianModel = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/gaussian_model.py', 'GaussianModel')
    ToneMapping = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/renderer/__init__.py', 'ToneMapping')
    training = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/training.py', 'training')
    global render
    render = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/renderer/__init__.py', 'render')
        
    # load information for all scenes within the scene_list:
    scene_dict = load_yaml_file('config_files/scene_info.yaml')

    if len(test_scene_list) == 1 and test_scene_list[0] == 'all':
        test_scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item]
    elif len(test_scene_list) == 1 and test_scene_list[0] == 'all_real':
        test_scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item and 'synthetic' not in item]
    elif len(test_scene_list) == 1 and test_scene_list[0] == 'all_synthetic':
        test_scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item and 'real' not in item]
    elif len(test_scene_list) == 1 and test_scene_list[0] == 'all_m360':
        test_scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'm360' in item]
    else:
        pass
    

    # setup the remaining parameters
    parser = ArgumentParser(description="Testing script parameters")
    model = ModelParams(parser)
    pipeline = PipelineParams(parser)
    parser.add_argument("--iteration", default=-1, type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--skip_train", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--skip_test", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--quiet", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(sys.argv[5:])


    for idx, scene_name in enumerate(test_scene_list):        
        try:
            conf.source_path = scene_dict[scene_name]['source_path']
            hold = scene_dict[scene_name].get('holdout', 8)
        except:
            print("Scene not found")
        conf.model_path = os.path.join('./output', conf.model_version, conf.model_folder, scene_name)

        # merge arguments and params specified within the training-time config file   
        cur_args = copy.deepcopy(args) 
        setattr(conf, 'hold', hold)
        conf, cur_args = merge_params(conf, cur_args)
        cur_args = get_combined_args(cur_args)
    
    
        if cur_args.iteration == -1:
            test_iteration = int(str(cur_args.iterations)) 
        else:
            test_iteration = cur_args.iteration

        print(f"Rendering {scene_name} from: " + cur_args.model_path)

        # Initialize system state (RNG)
        safe_state(cur_args.quiet)

        # Get band_limit with proper type handling
        band_limit = getattr(cur_args, 'band_limit', False)
        band_limit = False if str(band_limit).lower() == 'false' else bool(band_limit)
        # Get kernel_size with proper default
        kernel_size = getattr(conf, 'kernel_size', 0.1)
        render = partial(render, band_limit=band_limit, kernel_size=float(kernel_size))
        
        # render and compute metrics
        render_sets(GaussianModel, model.extract(cur_args), test_iteration, pipeline.extract(cur_args), 
                    cur_args.skip_train, cur_args.skip_test)
```

### train.py
```
#
# Copyright (C) 2023, Inria
# GRAPHDECO research group, https://team.inria.fr/graphdeco
# All rights reserved.
#
# This software is free for non-commercial, research and evaluation use 
# under the terms of the LICENSE.md file.
#
# For inquiries contact  george.drettakis@inria.fr
#

import os
import torch
from random import randint
import sys
from src.scene import Scene
from src.utils.general_utils import safe_state, import_class_from_file, load_yaml_file
import uuid
from tqdm import tqdm
from src.utils.image_utils import psnr
from argparse import ArgumentParser, Namespace
from omegaconf import OmegaConf
import omegaconf
from importlib import import_module
import json
import shutil 
import time

try:
    from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
    TENSORBOARD_FOUND = True
except ImportError:
    TENSORBOARD_FOUND = False
    


def backup_code(conf):
    if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(conf.model_path, 'backup_src')):
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(conf.model_path, 'backup_src'))
    shutil.copytree('./src', os.path.join(conf.model_path, 'backup_src'))
    


def merge_params(conf, args):
    for key, value in args.__dict__.items():
        if key in conf.__dict__['_content'].keys():
            print('In config file ', key, getattr(conf, key))
            setattr(args, key, getattr(conf, key))
        else:
            setattr(conf, key, value)
        print(key, getattr(args, key))
    
    for key, value in conf.__dict__['_content'].items():
        if key not in args.__dict__.keys():
            setattr(args, key, value)
    return conf, args
            
def prepare_output_and_logger(args):    
    if not args.model_path:
        if os.getenv('OAR_JOB_ID'):
            unique_str=os.getenv('OAR_JOB_ID')
        else:
            unique_str = str(uuid.uuid4())
        args.model_path = os.path.join("./output/", unique_str[0:10])
        
    # Set up output folder
    print("Output folder: {}".format(args.model_path))
    os.makedirs(args.model_path, exist_ok = True)
    with open(os.path.join(args.model_path, "cfg_args"), 'w') as cfg_log_f:
        cfg_log_f.write(str(Namespace(**vars(args))))

    # Create Tensorboard writer
    tb_writer = None
    if TENSORBOARD_FOUND:
        tb_writer = SummaryWriter(args.model_path)
    else:
        print("Tensorboard not available: not logging progress")
    return tb_writer



if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Set up command line argument parser
    parser_config = ArgumentParser(description="argparser for the config file")
    parser_config.add_argument('--config', type=str, required=True, help='config file')
    args_config = parser_config.parse_args(sys.argv[1:3])  # sys.argv[1:3] contains the config file path
    
    # load the config file
    conf = OmegaConf.load(args_config.config)
    
    # import the needed argument and model classes 
    ModelParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'ModelParams')
    PipelineParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'PipelineParams')
    OptimizationParams = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/arguments/__init__.py', 'OptimizationParams')
        
    # load model classes
    GaussianModel = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/gaussian_model.py', 'GaussianModel')
    training = import_class_from_file(f'src/{conf.model_version}/training.py', 'training')
    
    # load_scene_info:
    scene_dict = load_yaml_file('config_files/scene_info.yaml')
        
    # setup the remaining parameters
    parser = ArgumentParser(description="Training script parameters")
    lp = ModelParams(parser)
    op = OptimizationParams(parser)
    pp = PipelineParams(parser)
    parser.add_argument('--ip', type=str, default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument('--port', type=int, default=9933)
    parser.add_argument('--debug_from', type=int, default=-1)
    parser.add_argument('--detect_anomaly', action='store_true', default=False)
    parser.add_argument('--add_point_iterations', nargs="+", type=int, default=[2500])
    parser.add_argument("--test_iterations", nargs="+", type=int, default=[1_000, 3_000, 7_000, 30_000])
    parser.add_argument("--save_iterations", nargs="+", type=int, default=[1_000, 3_000, 7_000, 30_000])
    parser.add_argument("--quiet", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_iterations", nargs="+", type=int, default=[])
    parser.add_argument("--start_checkpoint", type=str, default = None)

    args = parser.parse_args(sys.argv[3:])
    
    if args.save_iterations[-1] != conf.iterations:
        args.save_iterations.append(conf.iterations)
    
    if args.test_iterations[-1] != conf.iterations:
        args.test_iterations.append(conf.iterations)
    

    if conf.scene_name == 'all':
        scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item]
    elif conf.scene_name == 'all_synthetic':
        scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item and 'real' not in item and 'synthetic_gt' not in item]
    elif conf.scene_name == 'all_real':
        scene_list = [item for item in scene_dict.keys() if 'bush' not in item and 'synthetic' not in item]
    else:
        scene_list = list(conf.scene_name) if isinstance(conf.scene_name, omegaconf.listconfig.ListConfig) else [conf.scene_name]           
    print('We will train following scenes:', '.'.join(scene_list))
 
    
    depth_estimator=None
    # create the table head for the final performance summary table
    md_table_head = "| Scene Name | Iteration | Train PSNR  | Train PSNR (fake)  | Train SSIM | Train SSIM (fake) | Test PSNR  | Test SSIM |\n|-------------|-----------------|-----------------|-----------------|-----------------|-----------------|-----------------|-----------------|\n"
    markdown_table = md_table_head

    
    for idx, scene_name in enumerate(scene_list):
        print(f'Start training {scene_name}==============================')

        # table head for the current scene
        cur_md_table = md_table_head
        conf.model_path = os.path.join('./output', conf.model_version, conf.model_folder, scene_name)

        try:
            conf.source_path = scene_dict[scene_name]['source_path']
            llffhold = scene_dict[scene_name].get('holdout', 8)
            setattr(conf, 'hold', llffhold)
        except:
            print(f'Data source for {scene_name} is not found, which is ', conf.source_path)    
        
        # merge arguments and params specified within the config file    
        conf, args = merge_params(conf, args)
        if 'infocus_range_list' in conf.__dict__['_content'].keys():
            args.infocus_range_list = [list(item) for item in list(conf.infocus_range_list)]
        

        # prepare the output folder and tensorboard writer
        tb_writer = prepare_output_and_logger(lp.extract(args))
        # backup source code folder into the model path (for reproducibility)
        backup_code(conf)
        # save the runtime config file to model path
        OmegaConf.save(config=conf, f=os.path.join(conf.model_path, 'config_runtime.yaml'))
        print("Optimizing " + args.model_path)

        # Initialize system state (RNG)
        safe_state(args.quiet)
        # Start GUI server, configure and run training
        #network_gui.init(args.ip, args.port)
        torch.autograd.set_detect_anomaly(args.detect_anomaly)
        
        start = time.time()
        return_numbers = training(GaussianModel, lp.extract(args), op.extract(args), pp.extract(args), args.test_iterations, args.save_iterations, 
                args.checkpoint_iterations, args.add_point_iterations, args.start_checkpoint, args.debug_from, tb_writer, depth_estimator)
        end = time.time()
        elapsed = end - start 
        minutes = elapsed / 60.0
        print(f'Training of {scene_name} took {minutes:.4f} minutes')


        # summarize the performance into a markdown table
        for iter_idx, iter in enumerate(conf.test_iterations):
            key = f'Iter-{iter:d}'
            if key in return_numbers.keys():
                cur_train_psnr, cur_test_psnr = return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['train_psnr'], return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['test_psnr']
                cur_train_ssim, cur_test_ssim = return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['train_ssim'], return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['test_ssim']
                
                if 'train_psnr-fake' not in return_numbers[key].keys():
                    if iter_idx == 0:
                        cur_row = f"| {scene_name} | {iter:d} | {cur_train_psnr: .4f} | | {cur_train_ssim: .4f} | |{cur_test_psnr: .4f} | {cur_test_ssim: .4f} |\n"
                    else:
                        cur_row = f"|  | {iter:d} | {cur_train_psnr: .4f} |  | {cur_train_ssim: .4f} |  |{cur_test_psnr: .4f} | {cur_test_ssim: .4f}  |\n"
                else:
                    train_psnr_fake, train_ssim_fake = return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['train_psnr-fake'], return_numbers[f'Iter-{iter:d}']['train_ssim-fake']
                    if iter_idx == 0:
                        cur_row = f"| {scene_name} | {iter:d} | {cur_train_psnr: .4f} |{train_psnr_fake: .4f} | {cur_train_ssim: .4f} | {train_ssim_fake: .4f}|{cur_test_psnr: .4f} | {cur_test_ssim: .4f} |\n"
                    else:
                        cur_row = f"|  | {iter:d} | {cur_train_psnr: .4f} | {train_psnr_fake: .4f}  | {cur_train_ssim: .4f} |{train_ssim_fake: .4f}  |{cur_test_psnr: .4f} | {cur_test_ssim: .4f}  |\n"
                
                markdown_table += cur_row
                cur_md_table += cur_row
              
        
        # Write into a markdown file
        with open(os.path.join(conf.model_path, "cur_perform.md"), "w") as f:
            f.write(cur_md_table)
         
        # All done
        print(f"\nTraining Scene {idx:d} complete.")

### src/math/mpi_math.py

import tensorflow as tf
import numpy as np
import os


ALPHA_EPS = 1e-10


##########################
#  Homography/matrix math for MPIs and plane sweep volumes
##########################
def tfmm(A, B):
    with tf.variable_scope('tfmm'):
        return tf.reduce_sum(A[..., :, :, tf.newaxis] * B[..., tf.newaxis, :, :], axis=-2)

        
# Converts my pose format into 4x4 extrinsic and 3x3 intrinsic matrices
#   My rotations are in [down, right, backwards] orientation, 
#   hence the 'fix_yx' thing to convert from that format [-y x z] to the more conventional [x y z]
def myposes2mats(poses, fix_yx=False):
    with tf.variable_scope('myposes2mats'):
        def cat(arrs, ax):
            return tf.concat(arrs, ax)
        def stk(arrs, ax):
            return tf.stack(arrs, ax)

        c2w = poses[..., :3, :4] + 0.
        bottom0 = tf.zeros_like(c2w[..., :1, :3])
        bottom1 = tf.ones_like(c2w[..., :1, 3:4])
        bottom = tf.concat([bottom0, bottom1], -1)
        # fix the -y x thing
        if fix_yx:
            c2w = cat([c2w[..., :3, 1:2], -c2w[..., :3, 0:1], c2w[..., :3, 2:]], -1)

        R = c2w[..., :3, :3] + 0. 
        t = c2w[..., :3, 3:4] + 0.
        T_c2w = cat([c2w, bottom], -2)

        N = len(R.get_shape().as_list())
        perm = list(range(N-2)) + [N-1, N-2]
        R_inv = tf.transpose(R, perm)
        t_inv = -tfmm(R_inv, t)
        T_w2c = cat([cat([R_inv, t_inv], -1), bottom], -2)

        h, w, f = poses[..., 0, -1], poses[..., 1, -1], poses[..., 2, -1]
        m_z, m_o = tf.zeros_like(h), tf.ones_like(h)

        sh = tf.shape(poses)[:-2]
        sh = tf.concat([sh, tf.constant([3]), tf.constant([3])], -1)
        with tf.variable_scope('Kstuff'):
            K = stk([f, m_z, -w*.5, 
                     m_z, f, -h*.5,
                     m_z, m_z, -m_o], -1)
            K = tf.reshape(K, sh)
            
            K_inv = stk([1./f, m_z, -w*.5/f,
                         m_z, 1./f, -h*.5/f,
                         m_z, m_z, -m_o], -1)
            K_inv = tf.reshape(K_inv, sh)
            
        T_c2w, T_w2c, K, K_inv = map(lambda x : tf.cast(x, dtype=poses.dtype), [T_c2w, T_w2c, K, K_inv])
        return T_c2w, T_w2c, K, K_inv





# To warp a single plane, for PSV creation and MPI rendering
def plane_homogs(pose_t, pose_s, depths, planes_from_t=True, y_flip=True, fix_yx=False):
    with tf.variable_scope('plane_homogs'):
        T_t2w, _, _, K_t_inv = myposes2mats(pose_t, fix_yx=fix_yx)
        _, T_w2s, K_s, _ = myposes2mats(pose_s, fix_yx=fix_yx)

        T_t2s = tfmm(T_w2s, T_t2w)
        R = T_t2s[..., tf.newaxis, :3,:3]
        t = T_t2s[..., tf.newaxis, :3, 3:4]
        n = tf.constant(np.array([0,0,1.]), dtype=T_t2s.dtype)
        n = tf.reshape(n, [3,1])
        nT = tf.transpose(n)
        a = tf.reshape(depths, [-1, 1, 1])

        if planes_from_t:
            H = R - tfmm(t, nT) / a
        else:
            H = R - tfmm(t, tfmm(nT, R)) / (a + tfmm(nT, t))

        H = tfmm(K_s[..., tf.newaxis,:,:], tfmm(H, K_t_inv[..., tf.newaxis,:,:]))
        if y_flip:
            premat = tf.stack([1,0,0., 0,-1,pose_t[0,-1]-1, 0,0,1.],-1)
            premat = tf.cast(premat, dtype=T_t2s.dtype)
            premat = tf.reshape(premat, [3,3])
            
            postmat = tf.stack([1,0,0., 0,-1,pose_s[0,-1]-1, 0,0,1.],-1)
            postmat = tf.cast(postmat, dtype=T_t2s.dtype)
            postmat = tf.reshape(postmat, [3,3])
            
            H = tfmm(postmat, tfmm(H, premat))
        return H
    
    

    
# Clone of tf.contrib.image.transform since that fn did not work on the RTX 2080 Ti
def homog_warp(img, H, retcos=False, window=None):
    with tf.variable_scope('homog_warp'):  
        sh = tf.shape(img)
        h, w = sh[-3], sh[-2]

        H = tf.reshape(tf.concat([H, tf.ones_like(H[:, :1])], -1), [-1,3,3])
        
        bds = tf.stack([0,0,h,w], 0)
        if window is not None:
            bds = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window, lambda : bds)
        coords = tf.meshgrid(tf.range(bds[0], bds[2]), tf.range(bds[1], bds[3]), indexing='ij')
        # coords = tf.meshgrid(tf.range(h), tf.range(w), indexing='ij')
        coords = tf.cast(tf.stack([coords[1], coords[0]], 0), H.dtype) # [2, H, W]

        coords_t = tf.concat([coords, tf.ones_like(coords[:1, ...])], 0) # [3, H, W]
        coords_t = tf.reshape(H, [-1,3,3,1,1]) * coords_t # [-1, 3, 3, H, W]
        coords_t = tf.reduce_sum(coords_t, -3) # [-1, 3, H, W]
        coords_t = coords_t[...,:2,:,:] / coords_t[...,-1:,:,:] # [-1, 2, H, W]
        
        warp = tf.transpose(coords_t, [0,2,3,1]) # [-1, H, W, 2]
        rect_tf = tf.squeeze(tf.contrib.resampler.resampler(img, warp))

        rets = rect_tf 
        if retcos:
            rets = [rect_tf, coords_t]
        return rets


# homog_render_fn = lambda data, homog : tf.contrib.image.transform(data, homog, interpolation='BILINEAR') 
homog_render_fn = lambda data, homog, window=None : homog_warp(data, homog, window=window)


def render_mpi_homogs(mpi_rgba, pose, newpose, min_disp, max_disp, num_depths, debug=False):  
    with tf.variable_scope('render_mpi_homogs'):  
        
        outs = {}
        dispvals = tf.linspace(min_disp, max_disp, num_depths)

        H = plane_homogs(newpose, pose, 1./dispvals, planes_from_t=False, y_flip=True, fix_yx=True)
        H = tf.reshape(H, [-1, 9])
        H = H[:, :8] / H[:, 8:]

        data_in = tf.transpose(tf.squeeze(mpi_rgba), [2,0,1,3])
        window = tf.cast([0,0,newpose[0,-1],newpose[1,-1]], tf.int32)
        mpi_reproj = homog_render_fn(data_in, H, window=window) 
        mpi_reproj = tf.transpose(mpi_reproj, [1,2,0,3])[tf.newaxis, ...]
        
        # back to front compositing 
        mpiR_alpha = mpi_reproj[..., 3:4] # 1 H W D 1
        mpiR_color = mpi_reproj[..., 0:3] # 1 H W D 3

        # Add small ALPHA_EPS to prevent gradient explosion nans from tf.cumprod derivative
        weights = mpiR_alpha * tf.cumprod(1.-mpiR_alpha + ALPHA_EPS, -2, exclusive=True, reverse=True) # goddamnit pratul
        alpha_acc = tf.reduce_sum(weights[..., 0], -1)
        rendering = tf.reduce_sum(weights * mpiR_color, -2)
        accum = tf.cumsum(weights * mpiR_color, -2, reverse=True)

        return rendering, alpha_acc, accum
    
    
# Create a plane sweep volume ('cost volume')
# vectorized on initial axes of img and pose
def make_psv_homogs(img, pose, newpose, dispvals, num_depths, window=None): 
    with tf.variable_scope('make_cv_homogs'):  

        sh = tf.shape(pose)[:-2] # [N, 3,4]
        H = plane_homogs(newpose, pose, 1./dispvals, planes_from_t=True, y_flip=True, fix_yx=True) # [N, D, 3, 3]
        H = tf.reshape(H, [-1, 9]) # [N*D, 9]
        H = H[:, :8] / H[:, 8:]
    
        img_sh = tf.shape(tf.squeeze(img))
        img = tf.reshape(img, [-1, 1, img_sh[-3], img_sh[-2], img_sh[-1]]) # [N, 1, H, W, 3] 
        img_tiled = tf.tile(img, [1, num_depths, 1, 1, 1]) # go in as [N, D, H, W, 3]
        img_tiled = tf.reshape(img_tiled, [-1, img_sh[-3], img_sh[-2], img_sh[-1]]) # [N*D, H, W, 3] 

        cvd = homog_render_fn(img_tiled, H, window=window) # come out as [N*D, H, W, 3]
        h, w = img_sh[-3], img_sh[-2]
        if window is not None:
            h = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[2]-window[0], lambda : h)
            w = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[3]-window[1], lambda : w)
        cvd = tf.reshape(cvd, [-1, num_depths, h, w, img_sh[-1]]) # [N, D, H, W, 3]
        cvd = tf.squeeze(tf.transpose(cvd, [2,3,1,4,0])) # [H, W, D, 3, N] or [H, W, D, 3]

        return cvd
    
    

    

##########################
#
#   3d projection math for traditional depth map backwards warping
#
##########################


class Pose:
    def __init__(self, m, window=None): #, h, w, focal, mat):
        self.h = m[0,4]
        self.w = m[1,4]
        self.f = m[2,4]
        self.mat = m[:3,:4]
        
        R = self.mat[:3, :3]
        t = self.mat[:3, 3]
        RT = tf.transpose(R)
        self.invmat = tf.concat([RT, -tf.reduce_sum(RT*t, axis=-1, keep_dims=True)], axis=-1)
        
        if window is None:
            w0, h0 = 0., 0.
            w1, h1 = self.w, self.h
        else:
            window = tf.cast(window, m.dtype)
            h0 = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[0], lambda : 0.)
            w0 = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[1], lambda : 0.)
            h1 = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[2], lambda : self.h) - h0
            w1 = tf.cond(window[3] > 0, lambda : window[3], lambda : self.w) - w0
            # w0, h0 = window[1], window[0]
            # w1, h1 = window[3], window[2]
        self.w0, self.h0, self.w1, self.h1 = w0, h0, w1, h1
        
        self.xf = (self.w-0) / self.f * .5
        self.yf = (self.h-0) / self.f * .5
        yvals = (tf.to_float(tf.range(h0, h0+h1)) - self.h*.5) / self.f
        xvals = (tf.to_float(tf.range(w0, w0+w1)) - self.w*.5) / self.f
        co_y, co_x = tf.meshgrid(yvals, xvals, indexing='ij')
        
        self.cam_co = tf.stack([co_y, co_x, -tf.ones_like(co_y)], axis=-1)
        
    def matmul(self, M, pts):
        pts = tf.concat([pts, tf.ones_like(pts[...,:1])], axis=-1)
        pts = tf.reduce_sum(M * tf.expand_dims(pts, -2), axis=-1)
        return pts

    def world2cam(self, pts):
        return self.matmul(self.invmat, pts)
    
    def cam2world(self, pts):
        return self.matmul(self.mat, pts)
        
        
    def project_out(self, depths, single=False):
        if not single:
            depths = tf.expand_dims(depths, axis=-1)
            
        pts = self.cam_co * depths
        pts = self.cam2world(pts)
        return pts
        
    
    def project_in(self, pts, clip=False):
        
        pts = self.world2cam(pts)
        pts = pts[..., :2] / -pts[..., 2:3]
        
        pts_y = (pts[...,0]/self.yf + 1.) * self.h * .5
        pts_x = (pts[...,1]/self.xf + 1.) * self.w * .5
        if clip:
            pts_y = tf.clip_by_value(pts_y, 0., self.h-1.)
            pts_x = tf.clip_by_value(pts_x, 0., self.w-1.)
        ret = tf.stack([pts_y, pts_x], axis=-1)

        return ret


def warp(img, pose, newpose, depth, single=False, debug=False):
    pts = newpose.project_out(depth, single)
    pix = pose.project_in(pts, clip=False)
    
    pix = tf.expand_dims(pix, 0)
    pix_y, pix_x = pix[...,0], pix[...,1]
    
    mask_y = tf.logical_and(pix_y <= pose.h-1., pix_y >= 0.)
    mask_x = tf.logical_and(pix_x <= pose.w-1., pix_x >= 0.)
    mask = tf.to_float(tf.logical_and(mask_x, mask_y))
    
    pix_y = tf.clip_by_value(pix_y, 0., pose.h-1.)
    pix_x = tf.clip_by_value(pix_x, 0., pose.w-1.)
    output = tf.contrib.resampler.resampler(img, tf.stack([pix_x, pix_y], -1))
    output_masked = tf.expand_dims(mask, -1) * output
    
    if debug:
        return output_masked, mask, pix, output
    else:
        return output_masked, mask
```

### src/math/pose_math.py
```
import sys
import os
import numpy as np

import matplotlib as mpl
mpl.use('agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
# mpl.use('Agg')


def normalize(x):
    return x / np.linalg.norm(x)

def viewmatrix(z, up, pos):
    vec2 = normalize(z)
    vec0_avg = up
    vec1 = normalize(np.cross(vec2, vec0_avg))
    vec0 = normalize(np.cross(vec1, vec2))
    m = np.stack([vec0, vec1, vec2, pos], 1)
    return m

def poses_avg(poses):

    hwf = poses[:3, -1:, 0]

    center = poses[:3, 3, :].mean(-1)
    vec2 = normalize(poses[:3, 2, :].sum(-1))
    vec0_avg = poses[:3, 0, :].sum(-1)
    c2w = np.concatenate([viewmatrix(vec2, vec0_avg, center), hwf], 1)
    
    return c2w

def ptstocam(pts, c2w):
    tt = np.matmul(c2w[:3,:3].T, (pts-c2w[:3,3])[...,np.newaxis])[...,0]
    return tt


def nearest_pose(p, poses):
    dists = np.sum(np.square(p[:3, 3:4] - poses[:3, 3, :]), 0)
    return np.argsort(dists)



def render_path_axis(c2w, up, ax, rad, focal, N):
    render_poses = []
    center = c2w[:,3]
    hwf = c2w[:,4:5]
    v = c2w[:,ax] * rad
    for t in np.linspace(-1.,1.,N+1)[:-1]:
        c = center + t * v
        z = normalize(c - (center - focal * c2w[:,2]))
        render_poses.append(np.concatenate([viewmatrix(z, up, c), hwf], 1))
    return render_poses
    
        
def render_path_spiral(c2w, up, rads, focal, zdelta, zrate, rots, N):
    render_poses = []
    rads = np.array(list(rads) + [1.])
    hwf = c2w[:,4:5]
    
    for theta in np.linspace(0., 2. * np.pi * rots, N+1)[:-1]:
        c = np.dot(c2w[:3,:4], np.array([-np.sin(theta), np.cos(theta), -np.sin(theta*zrate), 1.]) * rads) 
        z = normalize(c - np.dot(c2w[:3,:4], np.array([0,0,-focal, 1.])))
        render_poses.append(np.concatenate([viewmatrix(z, up, c), hwf], 1))
    return render_poses
    
    
def generate_render_path(poses, bds, comps=None, N=30):
    if comps is None:
        comps = [True]*5
    
    close_depth, inf_depth = bds[0, :].min()*.9, bds[1, :].max()*5.
    dt = .75
    mean_dz = 1./(((1.-dt)/close_depth + dt/inf_depth))
    focal = mean_dz
    
    shrink_factor = .8
    zdelta = close_depth * .2

    c2w = poses_avg(poses)
    up = normalize(poses[:3, 0, :].sum(-1))
    
    tt = ptstocam(poses[:3,3,:].T, c2w).T
    rads = np.percentile(np.abs(tt), 90, -1)

    render_poses = []
    
    if comps[0]:
        render_poses += render_path_axis(c2w, up, 1, shrink_factor*rads[1], focal, N)
    if comps[1]:
        render_poses += render_path_axis(c2w, up, 0, shrink_factor*rads[0], focal, N)
    if comps[2]:
        render_poses += render_path_axis(c2w, up, 2, shrink_factor*zdelta, focal, N)
    
    rads[2] = zdelta
    if comps[3]:
        render_poses += render_path_spiral(c2w, up, rads, focal, zdelta, 0., 1, N*2)
    if comps[4]:
        render_poses += render_path_spiral(c2w, up, rads, focal, zdelta, .5, 2, N*4)
    
    render_poses = np.array(render_poses)
    
    return render_poses



def render_path_fig(poses, render_poses, scaling_factor=1., savepath=None):
    c2w = poses_avg(poses)
    tt = pts2cam(poses, c2w)


    plt.figure(figsize=(12,4))

    plt.subplot(121)
    tt = ptstocam(render_poses[:,:3,3], c2w) * scaling_factor
    plt.plot(tt[:,1], -tt[:,0])
    tt = ptstocam(poses[:3,3,:].T, c2w) * scaling_factor
    plt.scatter(tt[:,1], -tt[:,0])
    plt.axis('equal')

    plt.subplot(122)
    tt = ptstocam(render_poses[:,:3,3], c2w) * scaling_factor
    plt.plot(tt[:,1], tt[:,2])
    tt = ptstocam(poses[:3,3,:].T, c2w) * scaling_factor
    plt.scatter(tt[:,1], tt[:,2])
    plt.axis('equal')

    if savepath is not None:
        plt.savefig(os.path.join(savepath, 'path_slices.png'))
        plt.close()
```

### src/metrics/__init__.py
```
from skimage import metrics
import torch
import torch.hub
import os
import numpy as np

photometric = {
    "mse": None,
    "ssim": None,
    "psnr": None,
}

def compute_img_metric(im1t: torch.Tensor, im2t: torch.Tensor,
                       metric="mse", margin=0, mask=None):
    """
    im1t, im2t: torch.tensors with batched imaged shape, range from (0, 1)
    """
    if metric not in photometric.keys():
        raise RuntimeError(f"img_utils:: metric {metric} not recognized")
    if photometric[metric] is None:
        if metric == "mse":
            photometric[metric] = metrics.mean_squared_error
        elif metric == "ssim":
            photometric[metric] = metrics.structural_similarity
        elif metric == "psnr":
            photometric[metric] = metrics.peak_signal_noise_ratio

    if mask is not None:
        if mask.dim() == 3:
            mask = mask.unsqueeze(1)
        if mask.shape[1] == 1:
            mask = mask.expand(-1, 3, -1, -1)
        mask = mask.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).numpy()
        batchsz, hei, wid, _ = mask.shape
        if margin > 0:
            marginh = int(hei * margin) + 1
            marginw = int(wid * margin) + 1
            mask = mask[:, marginh:hei - marginh, marginw:wid - marginw]

    # convert from [0, 1] to [-1, 1]
    im1t = (im1t * 2 - 1).clamp(-1, 1)
    im2t = (im2t * 2 - 1).clamp(-1, 1)

    if im1t.dim() == 3:
        im1t = im1t.unsqueeze(0)
        im2t = im2t.unsqueeze(0)
    im1t = im1t.detach().cpu()
    im2t = im2t.detach().cpu()

    if im1t.shape[-1] == 3:
        im1t = im1t.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        im2t = im2t.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)

    im1 = im1t.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).numpy()
    im2 = im2t.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).numpy()
    batchsz, hei, wid, _ = im1.shape
    if margin > 0:
        marginh = int(hei * margin) + 1
        marginw = int(wid * margin) + 1
        im1 = im1[:, marginh:hei - marginh, marginw:wid - marginw]
        im2 = im2[:, marginh:hei - marginh, marginw:wid - marginw]
    values = []

    for i in range(batchsz):
        if metric in ["mse", "psnr"]:
            if mask is not None:
                im1 = im1 * mask[i]
                im2 = im2 * mask[i]
            value = photometric[metric](
                im1[i], im2[i]
            )
            if mask is not None:
                hei, wid, _ = im1[i].shape
                pixelnum = mask[i, ..., 0].sum()
                value = value - 10 * np.log10(hei * wid / pixelnum)
        elif metric in ["ssim"]:
            value, ssimmap = photometric["ssim"](
                im1[i], im2[i], multichannel=True, full=True, channel_axis=-1, data_range=2,
            )
            if mask is not None:
                value = (ssimmap * mask[i]).sum() / mask[i].sum()
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError
        values.append(value)

    return sum(values) / len(values)
```
